## Narration 544

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A564)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي بِشْرُ بْنُ الْحَكَمِ الْعَبْدِيُّ، حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الْعَزِيزِ، - يَعْنِي الدَّرَاوَرْدِيَّ - عَنِ ابْنِ الْهَادِ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ إِبْرَاهِيمَ، عَنْ عِيسَى بْنِ طَلْحَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِذَا اسْتَيْقَظَ أَحَدُكُمْ مِنْ مَنَامِهِ فَلْيَسْتَنْثِرْ ثَلاَثَ مَرَّاتٍ فَإِنَّ الشَّيْطَانَ يَبِيتُ عَلَى خَيَاشِيمِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 564]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/564" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 564</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   564 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira reported: The Apostle of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said. When any one of you awakes up from sleep and performs ablution, he must clean his nose three times, for the devil spends the night in the interior of his nose</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 564]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/564" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 564</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   564 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">ईसा बिन तलहा ने हज़रत अबू हुरैरा (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत की की नबी (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: जब तुम में से कोई नींद से बेदार हो तो तीन बार नाक झारे, शैतान उसकी नाक के बांसों पर रात गुज़ारता है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 564]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/564" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 564</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   564 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عیسیٰ بن طلحہ نےحضرت ابو ہریرہ ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سے روایت کی کہ نبی ﷺ نے فرمایا : ’’جب تم میں سے کوئی نیند سے بیدار ہو تو تین ناک جھاڑے ، شیطان اس کی ناک کے بانسوں پر رات گزارتا ہے ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 564]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/564" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 564]</p>

## Narration 545

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A565)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا إِسْحَاقُ بْنُ إِبْرَاهِيمَ، وَمُحَمَّدُ بْنُ رَافِعٍ، قَالَ ابْنُ رَافِعٍ حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الرَّزَّاقِ، أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ جُرَيْجٍ، أَخْبَرَنِي أَبُو الزُّبَيْرِ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ جَابِرَ بْنَ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، يَقُولُ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ إِذَا اسْتَجْمَرَ أَحَدُكُمْ فَلْيُوتِرْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 565]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/565" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Jabir b. &#x27;Abdullah reported that he heard the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) say: When anyone wipes himself with pebbles (after answering the call of nature) he should do this odd number of times</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 565]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/565" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू ज़ुबैर ने हज़रत जाबिर बिन अब्दुल्लाह (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) को सुना, कह रहे थे, रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: तुम में से कोई शख्श जब ठोस चीज़ से इस्तिंजा करे तो ताक़ अदद में करे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 565]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/565" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابو زبیر نے حضرت جابر بن عبد اللہ ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ کو سنا ، کہہ رہے تھے ، رسول اللہ ﷺ نے فرمایا : ’’تم میں سے کوئی شخص جب ٹھوس چیز سے استنجا کرے تو طاق عدد میں کرے ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 565]</p>

## Narration 546

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A566)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا هَارُونُ بْنُ سَعِيدٍ الأَيْلِيُّ، وَأَبُو الطَّاهِرِ، وَأَحْمَدُ بْنُ عِيسَى، قَالُوا أَخْبَرَنَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ وَهْبٍ، عَنْ مَخْرَمَةَ بْنِ بُكَيْرٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ سَالِمٍ، مَوْلَى شَدَّادٍ قَالَ دَخَلْتُ عَلَى عَائِشَةَ زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَوْمَ تُوُفِّيَ سَعْدُ بْنُ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ فَدَخَلَ عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ أَبِي بَكْرٍ فَتَوَضَّأَ عِنْدَهَا فَقَالَتْ يَا عَبْدَ الرَّحْمَنِ أَسْبِغِ الْوُضُوءَ فَإِنِّي سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَقُولُ ‏ &quot;‏ وَيْلٌ لِلأَعْقَابِ مِنَ النَّارِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 566]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/566" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 566</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   566 ]</p>

</details>

<details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Salim, the freed slave of Shaddad, said: I came to &#x27;A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him), on the day when Sa&#x27;db. Abi Waqqas died. &#x27;Abd al-Rahman bin Abu Bakr also came there and he performed ablution in her presence. She (Hadrat &#x27;A&#x27;isha) said: Abd al-Rahman, complete the ablution as I heard the Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) say: Woe to the heels because of hell-fire</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 566]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/566" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 566</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   566 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">मखरामा बिन बोकेर ने अपने वालिद से हज़रात साद बिन आबि वक़्क़ास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) फौत हुए मैं रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की अहलिया हज़रात ऐसा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) की खिदमत में हाज़िर हुआ. अब्दुर रहमान बिन आबि बक्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) उनके यहाँ आये और उनके पास वुडू किया तो उन्हों ने फ़रमाया: अब्दुर रहमान! खूब अछी तरह वुडू करो क्यूंकि मैंने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को फरमाते हुए सुना था: (वुडू के पानी से तर न होने वाली) एड़ियों के लिए आग का अज़ाब है.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 566]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/566" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">مخرمہ بن بکیر نے اپنےوالد سے ، انہوں نے شداد کے آزاد کردہ غلام سالم سے روایت کی ، انہوں نے کہا : جس دن حضرت سعد بن ابی وقاص ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ فوت ہوئے میں رسول اللہ ﷺ کی اہلیہ حضرت عائشہ ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ کی خدمت میں حاضر ہوا ۔ عبد الرحمن بن ابی بکر ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ ان کے ہاں آئے اور ان کے پاس وضو کیا تو انہو ں نےفرمایا : عبدالرحمن ! خوب اچھی طرح وضو کرو کیونکہ میں نے رسو ل اللہ ﷺ کو فرماتے ہوئے سنا تھا : ’’ ( وضوکے پانی سےتر نہ ہونے والی ) ایڑیوں کےلیے آگ کا عذاب ہے ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 566]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/566" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Makhrama bin Bukair ne apne walid se, unho ne Shaddad ke azad karda ghulam Salim se riwayat ki, unho ne kaha: Jis din Hazrat Saad bin Abi Waqqas (Radi Allahu Anhu) faut hue main Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki ahliya Hazrat Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) ki khidmat mein hazir hua. Abdur Rahman bin Abi Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) unke yahan aaye aur unke paas wudu kiya to unho ne farmaya: Abdur Rahman! khoob achi tarah wudu karo kyunki maine Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko farmate hue suna tha: (Wudu ke pani se tar na hone wali) eriyon ke liye aag ka azab hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 566]</p>

## Narration 547

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A567)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي حَرْمَلَةُ بْنُ يَحْيَى، حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ وَهْبٍ، أَخْبَرَنِي حَيْوَةُ، أَخْبَرَنِي مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّ أَبَا عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، مَوْلَى شَدَّادِ بْنِ الْهَادِ حَدَّثَهُ أَنَّهُ، دَخَلَ عَلَى عَائِشَةَ فَذَكَرَ عَنْهَا عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِمِثْلِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 567]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/567" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">एक और सनद से मुहम्मद बिन अब्दुर रहमान ने शद्दाद बिन हाद के अज़ाद करदा घुलम अबु अब्दुल्लाह (सलीम) से रिवायत बयान की कि वो हज़रात ऐशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) की खिदमत में हाज़िर हुए और फिर उनसे रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) का मज़कूरा-इ-बाला फरमान नक़ल किया.</p>

## Narration 548

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A568)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ حَاتِمٍ، وَأَبُو مَعْنٍ الرَّقَاشِيُّ قَالاَ حَدَّثَنَا عُمَرُ بْنُ يُونُسَ، حَدَّثَنَا عِكْرِمَةُ بْنُ عَمَّارٍ، حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى بْنُ أَبِي كَثِيرٍ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنِي - أَوْ، حَدَّثَنَا - أَبُو سَلَمَةَ بْنُ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، حَدَّثَنِي سَالِمٌ، مَوْلَى الْمَهْرِيِّ قَالَ خَرَجْتُ أَنَا وَعَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ أَبِي بَكْرٍ، فِي جَنَازَةِ سَعْدِ بْنِ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ فَمَرَرْنَا عَلَى بَابِ حُجْرَةِ عَائِشَةَ فَذَكَرَ عَنْهَا عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم مِثْلَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 568]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/568" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 568</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   568 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Salim, the freed slave of Mahri, reported: I and &#x27;Abd al-Rahman bin Abu Bakr went out (in order to join) the funeral procession of Sa&#x27;d b. Abi Waqqas and passed by the door of the residence of &#x27;A&#x27;isha, and then he transmitted a hadith like this from her who (narrated it) from the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 568]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/568" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू सलमः बिन अब्दुर रहमान ने बयां किया की मेहरी के अज़ाद करदा घुलम सलीम ने मुझे बताया की मैं और अब्दुर रहमान बिन आबि बक्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) साद बिन आबि वक़्क़ास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के जनाजे के लिए निकले तो हम हज़रात ऐषा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) के हुजरे के दरवाज़े से गुज़रे (वहां ठहरे ने हज़रात ऐषा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) के हवाले से मज़कूरा-इ-बाला रिवायात सुनाई.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 568]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/568" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوسلمہ بن عبد الرحمن نے بیان کیا کہ مہری کے آزاد کردہ غلام سالم نے مجھے بتایا کہ میں اور عبدالرحمن بن ابی بکر ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سعدبن ابی وقاص ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ کے جنازے کے لیے نکلے تو ہم حضرت عائشہ ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ کےحجرے کے دروازے سے گزرے ( وہاں ٹھہرے ، وضو کے لیے عبدالرحمان بن ابی بکر اندر گئے .... ) پھر انہوں نے حضرت عائشہ ؓ کےحوالے سے مذکورہ بالا روایت سنائی ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 568]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/568" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Salamah bin Abdur Rahman ne bayan kiya ki Mehri ke azad karda ghulam Salim ne mujhe bataya ki main aur Abdur Rahman bin Abi Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) Saad bin Abi Waqqas (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke janaze ke liye nikle to hum Hazrat Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) ke hujre ke darwaze se guzre (wahan thehre, wudu ke liye Abdur Rahman bin Abi Bakr andar gaye...) phir unho ne Hazrat Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) ke hawaley se mazkoora-e-bala riwayat sunai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 568]</p>

## Narration 549

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A569)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي سَلَمَةُ بْنُ شَبِيبٍ، حَدَّثَنَا الْحَسَنُ بْنُ أَعْيَنَ، حَدَّثَنَا فُلَيْحٌ، حَدَّثَنِي نُعَيْمُ بْنُ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنْ سَالِمٍ، مَوْلَى شَدَّادِ بْنِ الْهَادِ قَالَ كُنْتُ أَنَا مَعَ، عَائِشَةَ - رضى الله عنها - فَذَكَرَ عَنْهَا عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِمِثْلِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 569]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/569" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 569</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   569 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Salim, the freed slave of Shaddad b. al-Had said: I was in the presence of &#x27;A&#x27;isha, and then narrated on her authority a hadith like this from the Prophet (way peace be upon him)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 569]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/569" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">नाईं बिन अब्दुल्लाह ने शद्दाद बिन अल-हाड के आज़ाद करदह घुलम सालिम से रिवायत की (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) के पास था... फिर उसने हज़रात ऐषा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) से नबी (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से इसी तरह रिवायत बयान की.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 569]</p>

## Narration 550

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A570)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي زُهَيْرُ بْنُ حَرْبٍ، حَدَّثَنَا جَرِيرٌ، ح وَحَدَّثَنَا إِسْحَاقُ، أَخْبَرَنَا جَرِيرٌ، عَنْ مَنْصُورٍ، عَنْ هِلاَلِ بْنِ يِسَافٍ، عَنْ أَبِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَمْرٍو، قَالَ رَجَعْنَا مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم مِنْ مَكَّةَ إِلَى الْمَدِينَةِ حَتَّى إِذَا كُنَّا بِمَاءٍ بِالطَّرِيقِ تَعَجَّلَ قَوْمٌ عِنْدَ الْعَصْرِ فَتَوَضَّئُوا وَهُمْ عِجَالٌ فَانْتَهَيْنَا إِلَيْهِمْ وَأَعْقَابُهُمْ تَلُوحُ لَمْ يَمَسَّهَا الْمَاءُ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ وَيْلٌ لِلأَعْقَابِ مِنَ النَّارِ أَسْبِغُوا الْوُضُوءَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 570]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/570" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah b. &#x27;Amr reported: We returned from Mecca to Medina with the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him), and when we came to some water on the way, some of the people were in a hurry at the time of the afternoon prayer and performed ablution hurriedly; and when we reached them, their heels were dry, no water had touched them. The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said: Woe to (dry) heels, because of Hell-fire. Make your ablution thorough</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 570]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/570" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 570</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   570 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">जरीर ने मंसूर से, उन्होंने हिलाल बिन यासफ़ से, उन्होंने अबू यह्या (मुसद्दअ, अल-अराज) से और उन्होंने हज़रत अब्दुल्लाह बिन अम्र (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत की के उन्होंने कहा: हम रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के साथ मक्का से मदीना वापस आए। रास्ते में जब हम एक पानी (वाली मंज़िल) पर पहुंचे तो अस्र के वक़्त कुछ लोगों ने जल्दी की, वुज़ू किया तो जल्दी में थे, हम उन तक पहुंचे तो उनकी एड़ियां (heels) इस तरह नज़र आ रही थीं के उन्हें पानी नहीं लगा था, रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने (आकर) फ़रमाया: &#x27;इन एड़ियों के लिए आग का अज़ाब है। वुज़ू अच्छी तरह किया करो।&#x27;</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 570]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/570" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">جریر نے منصور سے ، انہوں نے ہلال بن یساف سے ، انہوں نے ابو یحییٰ ( مصدع ، الاعرج ) سے اور انہوں نے حضرت عبد اللہ بن عمرو ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سے روایت کی کہ انہوں نے کہا : ہم رسول اللہ ﷺ کے ساتھ مکہ سے مدینہ واپس آئے ۔ راستے میں جب ہم ایک پانی ( والی منزل ) پر پہنچے تو عصر کے وقت کچھ لوگوں نے جلدی کی ، وضو کیا تو جلدی میں تھے ، ہم ان تک پہنچے تو ان کی ایڑیاں اس طر ح نظر آ رہی تھیں کہ انہیں پانی نہیں لگا تھا ، رسول اللہ ﷺ نے ( آکر ) فرمایا : ’’ ( ان ) ایڑیوں کے لیے آگ کا عذاب ہے ۔ وضو اچھی طرح کیا کرو ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 570]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/570" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Jareer ne Mansoor se, unhone Hilal bin Yasaf se, unhone Abu Yahya (Musadda&#x27;, al-A&#x27;raj) se aur unhone Hazrat Abdullah bin Amr (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat ki ke unhone kaha: Hum Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke saath Makkah se Madina wapas aaye. Raaste mein jab hum ek paani (wali manzil) par pahunche toh Asr ke waqt kuch logon ne jaldi ki, wuzu kiya toh jaldi mein the, hum un tak pahunche toh unki eidiyan (heels) is tarah nazar aa rahi thin ke unhe paani nahi laga tha, Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne (aakar) farmaya: &#x27;In eidiyon ke liye aag ka azaab hai. Wuzu achi tarah kiya karo.&#x27;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 570]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/570" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
